# The Plant Protection Products (Miscellaneous Amendments) (EU Exit) Regulations 2019, SCHEDULE 2

> Text as at 2023-12-31.
>
> Extent: England, Wales, Scotland, Northern Ireland.

## SCHEDULE 2 — Revocations

*Extent: E+W+S+N.I.*

Regulation 29(1)

### PART 1 — Regulations
